- **memhub-staging**: STAGING build of the MemHub plugin — pointed at api.staging.memhub.xtrace.ai. For XTrace developers only; installs from your local clone, so it reflects whatever branch you have checked out (use `staging`). A path source is required here, not git-subdir: this plugin's skills/hooks/scripts are symlinks into ../memhub/, and a git-subdir fetch pulls only its own subdirectory, leaving them dangling. Registers an MCP server named `memhub`, so it collides with the public `memhub` plugin: install one or the other, never both. ([XTraceAI/agent-plugins](https://github.com/XTraceAI/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/memhub-staging))

Calibrated prediction & behavior discovery available in hosted mode. ([ThinkfleetAI/memmesh](https://github.com/ThinkfleetAI/memmesh/tree/HEAD/integrations/memmesh-plugin))

- **memoriant-patent-skills**: AI-powered patent workflow for Claude Code. 6 skills + 4 specialized agents that cover the full patent lifecycle: prior art search (USPTO 7-step methodology across PatentsView and USPTO databases), patentability analysis (35 USC 101, 102, 103, 112), application drafting (provisional, non-provisional, PCT formats), USPTO compliance review, patent diagram generation, and end-to-end pipeline orchestration. Model-agnostic — works with Claude, Ollama, vLLM, or any OpenAI-compatible endpoint. No servers required.
